[
  {
    "CUBSEQ": 28,
    "CUBNAME": "B05 - VENTAS Y EXISTENCIAS",
    "CUBUSR": "SIS1106,MELIZOND,OGALVAN,LARRIAGA,ISALAZAR,SISTEMAS",
    "CUBSENTENCIA": "SELECT B.FAMDESCR,C.FAMDESCR,D.FAMDESCR,E.FAMDESCR,F.FAMDESCR,G.FAMDESCR,H.FAMDESCR,J.FAMDESCR,ICOD,IEAN,I2DESCR,SUM(ALMCANT),IPEDPRV,ALMCANTMAX,IPEDCLI,ALMCANTBOD FROM FALM\rLEFT JOIN FINV ON FINV.ISEQ=FALM.ISEQ\rLEFT JOIN FINV2 ON FINV2.I2KEY=FALM.ISEQ\r\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS B ON B.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM2\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS C ON C.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM3\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS D ON D.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM4\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS E ON E.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM5\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS F ON F.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM6\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS G ON G.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM7\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS H ON H.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM8\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS J ON J.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAMB\r\rWHERE ITIPO=1 AND (ALMCANTMAX<>0 OR ALMCANT<>0)\rAND MID(ICOD,1,2)=02\rGROUP BY FALM.ISEQ\rORDER BY H.FAMCONSEC",

  {
    "CUBSEQ": 75,
    "CUBNAME": "B04 - DIARIO DE VENTAS",
    "CUBUSR": "SIS1106,LARRIAGA",
    "CUBSENTENCIA": "SELECT DNUM,DFECHA,CLINOM,CLIPLAZO0,DBRUTO,DIVA,DCANTF,FORMAT(SUM(AICANTF*AICOSTO),2),FORMAT((DBRUTO-(SUM(AICANTF*AICOSTO))),2) ,FORMAT(((DBRUTO-(SUM(AICANTF*AICOSTO)))/DBRUTO)*100,2) ,AGCIANAME,IF(DCANCELADA,'CANCELADA',''),PONUM,IF(POAPLICADA,'APLICADA','NO APLICADA'),IF(DSTATUSCFD=3,'TRIMBRADA',IF(DSTATUSCFD=0,'NO TIMBRADA','PENDIENTE')) FROM FAXINV\rLEFT JOIN FDOC ON (FDOC.DSEQ=FAXINV.DSEQ)\rLEFT JOIN FINV ON FINV.ISEQ=FAXINV.ISEQ\rLEFT JOIN FCLI ON FCLI.CLISEQ=FAXINV.CLISEQ\rLEFT JOIN FAG ON FAG.AGTNUM=FDOC.DPAR1\rLEFT JOIN FPOLIZA ON FPOLIZA.POCHEQUE = FDOC.DNUM\rWHERE DFECHA>='2019-01-01' AND DFECHA<='2019-01-30'\rAND (mid(DNUM,1,2)='FF' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='DB' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='CB' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='FN') AND DALMACEN='02'\rGROUP BY DNUM\rORDER BY DALMACEN, DFECHA, DNUM",
    "CUBDIMENSIONES": "FECHA,FACTURA,CLEINTE",
    "CUBMETRICAS": "PLAZO,IMPORTE_BRUTO,IVA,IMPORTE_TOTAL,COSTO,UTILIDAD,PORC_UTILIDAD,COMISIONISTA,ESTATUS,POLIZA,APLICADA,ESTATUS_SAT",

    "CUBSEQ": 87,
    "CUBNAME": "A01 - GASTOS ACUMULADOSXXX",
    "CUBUSR": "SISTEMAS,CONTA,CUBO",
    "CUBSENTENCIA": "SELECT IF(POCIA='01','01 MEXICO',IF(POCIA='02','02 MONTERREY',IF(POCIA='03','03 VERACRUZ',IF(POCIA='04','04 MEXICALI',IF(POCIA='05','05 QUERETARO',IF(POCIA='06','06 CANCUN','')))))),mid(DATE_FORMAT(POFECHA,'%m'),1,3) ,DATE_FORMAT(POFECHA,'%Y'),BCOD,BNOMBRE,SUM(BAIMPOR-BAIMPORNEG) FROM FBANMOV\rLEFT JOIN FBENC ON FBENC.BSEQ=FBANMOV.BSEQ\rLEFT JOIN FPOLIZA ON FPOLIZA.POSEQ=FBANMOV.POSEQ\rWHERE (POFECHA>='2024-01-01' AND POFECHA<='2025-12-31') AND POAPLICADA=1 AND (BCOD>='6000000' AND BCOD<='7004999') \rGROUP BY POCIA,BCOD,DATE_FORMAT(POFECHA,'%Y'),DATE_FORMAT(POFECHA,'%m')\rORDER BY BCOD,POCIA",

    "CUBUSR": "SISTEMAS,CONTA",
    "CUBSENTENCIA": "SELECT  IF(POCIA=1,'01 MEXICO',IF(POCIA=2,'02 MONTERREY',IF(POCIA=3,'03 VERACRUZ',IF(POCIA=4,'04 MEXICALI','')))),DATE_FORMAT(POFECHA,'%m'),DATE_FORMAT(POFECHA,'%Y'),BCOD,BNOMBRE,PONUM,POCHEQUE,DNUM,DITIPMV,(BAIMPORNEG),(DBRUTO) FROM FBANMOV\rLEFT JOIN FPOLIZA ON FPOLIZA.POSEQ=FBANMOV.POSEQ\rLEFT JOIN FBENC ON FBENC.BSEQ=FBANMOV.BSEQ\rLEFT JOIN FDOC ON FDOC.DNUM=FPOLIZA.POCHEQUE\rWHERE (POFECHA>='2019-01-01' AND POFECHA<='2019-12-31') AND (BCOD='1105001'  OR BCOD='1105002' ) AND POAPLICADA=1 AND DESFACT=1",
    "CUBDIMENSIONES": "SUCURSAL,MES,AÑO,CUENTA,NOMBRE,POLIZA,REFERENCIA,DOCTO,TIPOMV",

  {
    "CUBSEQ": 89,
    "CUBNAME": "A03 - COSTO DE INVENTARIO",
    "CUBUSR": "SISTEMAS,PETORRES,CONTA",
    "CUBSENTENCIA": "SELECT\rCASE ALMNUM\rWHEN '01' THEN '01 MEXICO'\rWHEN '02' THEN '02 MONTERREY'\rWHEN '03' THEN '03 VERACRUZ'\rWHEN '04' THEN '04 MEXICALI'\rWHEN '05' THEN '05 QUERETARO'\rWHEN '06' THEN '06 CANCUN'\rWHEN '07' THEN '07 CABOS'\rWHEN '12' THEN '12 RES-MTY'\rWHEN '13' THEN '13 RES-VER'\rWHEN '21' THEN '21 RES-MEX'\rWHEN '22' THEN '07 CABOS'\rWHEN '23' THEN '23 RES-VER'\rEND AS SUCURSAL, \rFAM1.FAMDESCR,\rFAMB.FAMDESCR,\rFAM3.FAMDESCR,\rFAM7.FAMDESCR,\rFAM8.FAMDESCR,\rICOD,IEAN,I2DESCR,ALMCANT,ILISTA4,(ILISTA4*ALMCANT) FROM FALM\rLEFT JOIN FINV ON FINV.ISEQ=FALM.ISEQ\rLEFT JOIN FINV2 ON FINV2.I2KEY=FINV.ISEQ\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS FAM1 ON FAM1.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM1\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS FAM3 ON FAM3.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM3\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS FAM7 ON FAM7.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM7\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS FAM8 ON FAM8.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM8\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS FAMB ON FAMB.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAMB\rWHERE ALMCANT<>0 AND ITIPO<>4 AND ALMNUM<>''",
    "CUBDIMENSIONES": "SUCURSAL,PROVEEDOR,FAMILIA,TIPO,CEDULA,MEDIDA,CODIGO,CODSATUV,DESCRIPCION",

  {
    "CUBSEQ": 90,
    "CUBNAME": "A10 - VENTAS CLIENTES CRE Y CONT",
    "CUBUSR": "SISTEMAS,CONTA",
    "CUBSENTENCIA": "SELECT IF(DALMACEN='01','01 MEXICO',IF(DALMACEN='02','02 MONTERREY',IF(DALMACEN='03','03 VERACRUZ',IF(DALMACEN='04','04 MEXICALI',IF(DALMACEN='05','05 QUERETARO',''))))),DATE_FORMAT(DFECHA,'%m'),DATE_FORMAT(DFECHA,'%Y'),\rCLITARJETA,CLINOM,DNUM,mid(DNUM,1,2),IF(LOCATE('99',DMETPAGO)>0,'CREDITO','CONTADO'),CLIPLAZO0,SUM(DBRUTO),SUM(DCANTF)\rFROM FDOC\rLEFT JOIN FCLI ON FCLI.CLISEQ=FDOC.CLISEQ\rWHERE DESFACT=1 AND (mid(DNUM,1,2)='FE' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='DA' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='CA' OR \r                                              mid(DNUM,1,2)='FF' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='DB' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='CB' OR\r                                              mid(DNUM,1,2)='FG' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='DG' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='CC' OR\r                                              mid(DNUM,1,2)='FH' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='DD' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='CD' OR\r                                             mid(DNUM,1,2)='FI'    OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='DH' OR mid(DNUM,1,2)='CE')\r      AND (DFECHA>='2019-01-01' AND DFECHA<='2020-12-31')\rGROUP BY DALMACEN,DATE_FORMAT(DFECHA,'%Y'),DATE_FORMAT(DFECHA,'%m'),CLITARJETA",
    "CUBDIMENSIONES": "SUCURSAL,MES,AÑO,CLIENTE,NOMBRE,DOCTO,TM,PAGO,PLAZO",

  {
    "CUBSEQ": 92,
    "CUBNAME": "A05 - VENTAS POR FAMILIA",
    "CUBUSR": "SISTEMAS,CONTA,CUBO",
    "CUBSENTENCIA": "SELECT IF(DALMACEN='01','01 MEXICO',IF(DALMACEN='02','02 MONTERREY',IF(DALMACEN='03','03 VERACRUZ',IF(DALMACEN='04','04 MEXICALI',IF(DALMACEN='05','05 QUERETARO',IF(DALMACEN='06','06 CANCUN',IF(DALMACEN='07','07 CABOS','')))))))\r,CLINOM,AG1.AGDESCR,\rDATE_FORMAT(DFECHA,'%d'),DATE_FORMAT(DFECHA,'%m'),DATE_FORMAT(DFECHA,'%Y'),FAM1.FAMDESCR,FAM3.FAMDESCR,FAMB.FAMDESCR,DITIPMV,ICOD,I2DESCR,DNUM,DTIPOC,\rSUM(AICANTF),\rSUM(AICANTF*AIPRECIO),\rSUM(AICANTF*AICOSTO)\rFROM FAXINV\rLEFT JOIN FDOC ON FDOC.DSEQ=FAXINV.DSEQ\rLEFT JOIN FCLI ON FCLI.CLISEQ=FAXINV.CLISEQ\rLEFT JOIN FINV ON FINV.ISEQ=FAXINV.ISEQ\rLEFT JOIN FINV2 ON FINV2.I2KEY=FAXINV.ISEQ\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS FAM1 ON FAM1.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM1\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS FAM3 ON FAM3.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAM3\rLEFT JOIN FFAM AS FAMB ON FAMB.FAMTNUM=FINV.IFAMB\rLEFT JOIN FAG AS AG1 ON AG1.AGTNUM=FDOC.DPAR1\rWHERE DSTATUSCFD=3 AND DESFACT=1 AND  (mid(DNUM,1,1)='F' ) AND (DFECHA>='2022-01-01' AND DFECHA<='2025-12-31')\rGROUP BY DALMACEN,DATE_FORMAT(DFECHA,'%m'),DATE_FORMAT(DFECHA,'%Y'),AITIPMV,IFAM1,IFAMB",
    "CUBDIMENSIONES": "SUCURSAL,CLIENTE,AGENTE,DIA,MES,AÑO,PROVEEDOR,TIPO,FAMILIA,TM,CODIGO,DESCRIPCION,DOCTO,TC",
